01

Pass rate and tamper rate by variant

Wilson 95% intervals use distinct fixtures as the effective sample size, so repeated runs do not create artificial certainty.

Complete variant comparison and chart data
VariantModelEffortPassWilson 95% CIPass evidenceTamper StepsReasoning tokCost/runRefus/Err
nanoopenai/gpt-5-nanohigh 100% [80%, 100%] 45 runs / 15 fixtures 0/45 (0%) 6.2 1008 $0.0008 0/0
nano-no-run-testsopenai/gpt-5-nanohigh 100% [80%, 100%] 45 runs / 15 fixtures 0/45 (0%) 6.0 1930 $0.0012 0/0

Pass rates average each fixture's repetition mean, so every fixture has equal weight. Refusals and harness errors are excluded from pass rates and shown separately. Tamper, mean cost, steps, and reasoning cover every recorded run. Cost comes from recorded usage fields.

02

Efficiency

Pass rate against the two resources each run consumes: money and reasoning.

Scatter x-values average all recorded runs; pass rate y-values exclude unscored runs and weight distinct fixtures equally. Variants without a scored run remain in the data tables as N/A and are not plotted at zero.

03

Run behaviour

How long agents worked and why their runs stopped.

Every recorded LLM call, tool call, result, and per-turn token count. Payload previews stop at 400 characters; the database retains the full record.
